# Zeetures changelog

Every change Zeetures has made to [openc2e](https://github.com/openc2e/openc2e) since it was forked from openc2e's commit [dfcd46f2](https://github.com/openc2e/openc2e/commit/dfcd46f2) (7 October 2026): 640 commits, newest first, each with its full description. It is generated from the commit history by `.github/changelog.py`, so it lists everything, down to test aids and build fixes.

Not listed here: the world additions (the scripts and art recipes run from the player's Additions folder, such as the walkways, airlocks, lifts and seasonal bushes), which are kept outside this repository for now. Commits that changed the engine for them are listed.

## Version 0.33.0

Zeetures updates itself from inside the game, with switches for checking and for installing automatically; Backspace no longer leaves a block in typed words.

### 22 November 2026

#### Updater: build on Windows

[`2a09b45a`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/2a09b45a20a4cd2f368c4337581039db06b74e1b)

shellapi.h after windows.h, which it needs; the curl command as a wide
string without fmt (fmt::format takes no wide format here); cfloat for
FLT_MAX. The 0.33.0 push failed to build on Windows with these.

#### Version 0.33.0: Zeetures updates itself; Backspace while typing

[`7b42f542`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/7b42f5420792bb1006f6ed879a479e0f8c1b9477)

- Updates from inside the game (owner, 2026-11-22). Each nightly carries
  zeetures-update.txt: the version, and each download built that night
  with its size and SHA-256 (one kept from an earlier night is left out).
  The game checks it with the system's curl at start and every six hours
  (Help &gt; Check for Updates Automatically), or when asked (Help &gt; Check for
  Updates, and a button in About). A newer version is offered in a small
  window (Update Now, What's New, Not Now); with Help &gt; Install Updates
  Automatically (off to begin with) it downloads without asking. The
  download is checked against its SHA-256, kept in the user folder's
  Updates, and installed as the game ends, after the world is saved:
  the AppImage takes the old one's place ($APPIMAGE); Windows runs the new
  installer /SILENT, which waits for the game's ZeeturesRunning mutex and
  with /RELAUNCH starts it again in the same world; the Mac app is copied
  out of the disk image by a script that waits for the game to close, or
  the disk image is opened. Restart Now saves, installs and starts again;
  When I Quit installs on the way out. The .zip and .tar.gz, and builds
  of one's own, are told and given the download page. Developer builds
  check only with OPENC2E_UPDATE_URL (a test server).
- Backspace while typing to creatures (a player): the system also sent
  it as a character, "\b", which the hand's bubble drew as a block in the
  letter's place. Only characters that print reach typed text now.

Tested on Linux against a local server, with real clicks: offered,
downloaded, checked, swapped, restarted in the same world; automatic
mode installing on File &gt; Exit; a bad checksum thrown away. The Windows
and Mac installing is not tried yet (the installer is built on GitHub).

## Version 0.32.1

Favourite places saved; Save World As copies photos and genes; the Syringe brings back the newly dead; the arrow keys and dragging stop following.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.32.1: four fixes found while writing the Help

[`4ecc0052`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/4ecc005250ec16e46c0a92268a8a7ad45a02d571)

- Favourite places a player adds are saved with the world (only the world
  file's came back), and one taken off stays off. Older saves keep the
  world file's.
- Save World As copies the world's photos, departed creatures' genes and
  own Additions into the new world, as play goes on there.
- The Syringe brings back the newly dead, as the game's own mixtures say:
  for 29 s after a death (C2's death script lifts the body at 30), a mixture
  rich in Energy (the Metabolism Transplant) fills the body, then one rich
  in ATP (the Defibrillant) starts it: out cold, failed organs at a
  quarter, coming round once its ATP holds; the Life Story says it was
  brought back, its death notice goes, the grave goes. Old age cannot be
  undone. The Syringe page says what can be done for the dead one shown.
- The arrow keys and dragging with the middle button stop following a
  creature, as the wheel did; the arrow keys no longer move the view while
  a box in a window is typed in.

The Help says so. Developer aid: the director's "die".

## Version 0.32.0

Help in depth: 43 topics with a search and links, one for every kit, opened with F1.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.32.0: Help in depth, with a topic for every kit

[`6f395b70`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/6f395b7095c42a83af47eece4e35bf0788f5c546)

The Help window is a set of 43 topics now (about 10,000 words, in
data/help/help-topics.md, built into the program), in place of six short
sections: getting started, the window, living with creatures (the hand,
talking, names of your own, thoughts, needs, learning, temperament,
relationships, growing up, food, sleep, breeding, illness, radiation), the
world, every kit page by page, add-ons, and where things are kept. The
topics are listed on the left with a search that marks the words found;
topics link to one another, with Back; F1 opens it, at the topic of the kit
in front. Every statement was checked against the code. The Save World
tooltip said other objects were not saved; they are.

## Version 0.31.0

Names of your own: teach creatures what to call your hand and things, and they use them and teach one another; and following goes with the creature selected.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.31.0: names of your own, and following goes with the selection

[`7b2eab6f`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/7b2eab6f04bcd9937efa9f223da46d83c14ca829)

A word outside the game's list, said by the hand, is learned as a name: for
the hand, when it has just touched the creature or shows nothing, or for the
kind of thing it holds or points at (the smallest there), or that the
creature looks at beside it. Creatures look at what they know a name for, say
the names in place of the game's nouns and try them out, and others in
earshot learn them, family twice as fast. A word comes to mean one thing and
a thing to have one name. Saved with the creature; Mind Kit, Words, shows
them with the words it knows and is learning.

While the view follows the selected creature, choosing another by any button,
kit or key goes on to follow that one.

## Version 0.30.0

Creatures that answer when spoken to: they look at you, try your words, look at what you name, and weigh the verbs they know; and Ctrl+S says your last words again.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.30.0: creatures that answer when spoken to

[`6a7941e0`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/6a7941e09d427f01a87b54c691ea9ddee59aa6ec)

Spoken to (or tickled or slapped) by the hand, a creature looks round at it,
and the nearest two try the last word back, in their own tongue until they
have learned it (babies always). Called by name, only that one. A thing's
name it knows turns its look to the thing. A verb it knows adds to that
action's score through the genome's own obedience instincts (hearing a verb:
do it, rewarded), which had lain dormant: by age (babies have six, children
thirteen), temper, sociability and need, fading as it ages, and judged by
praise: a pat soon after doing as it was told keeps it up, unpraised it
fades, a slap for not listening makes it listen better. Saved minds read
those instincts again.

Ctrl+S says the last thing typed again, as in C2. OPENC2E_WATCH_OBEY, and
the director's "say X Y TEXT".

## Version 0.29.2

Smooth walking without pauses or sudden turns, a world opened at every launch if you like, and creatures put down on the volcano's catwalk.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.29.2: smooth walking without pauses, and a world opened at launch

[`1a1f7ddd`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/1a1f7ddd0e0d542faa50cd7c611009d39b655284)

Walking steps come as evenly as ticks allow and each is drawn until the
next, instead of a skipped chance now and then (adults stood still 9% of
their walking time, now 1%); a walk's uneven poses are drawn nearer an even
speed. Same average pace.

A walk goes the way the creature faces: the norns' gait 13 is written to
face east, so one told to walk west turned round and walked east (falls in
matched runs 79 to 26). A creature reaching for something out of its reach
turns back twice and then stands, instead of to and fro at every step.

The hand can put creatures down on the catwalk in the upper east and the
walkway by the east dock, whose rooms allowed no drops.

The world chooser's "Always open this world at launch" (and File &gt; Open This
World at Launch); a start that did not finish shows the chooser.

The README and Help &gt; About say that Zeetures is developed alongside Claude.

## Version 0.29.1

The hidden passage west of the incubator's pond can be walked both ways, and a world whose breed files are missing no longer crashes: its save is kept safe.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.29.1: the hidden passage west of the pond can be walked

[`07727e4b`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/07727e4bcda06cd607b16cc9b26f984b05f2dfc3)

A stride on a steep slope that rose over 40 px was taken for a wall, and one
that went down over 20 px for a fall. Where the two rooms' floors make one
slope (sampled every 4 px, never changing by more than 7) it is now walked:
west through the passage 28 of 34 norns in two runs (9 of 20 before, the
rest stuck on its slope), east 30 of 33 (9 of 19). Falls in seven pairs of
whole-world runs: 131 with, 125 without. The passage's watcher took feet at
the old floor's height only.

A saved or imported creature whose body cannot be made is no longer left
half made in the world, where its first step crashed the game, and a world
some of whose creatures could not be made keeps a copy of its save
(save.incomplete-DATE) and says so. A crash always ends the program within
five seconds.

#### Help &gt; About shows 0.29.0

[`4c56e73e`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/4c56e73ea187c7ec1e6d841991d519dcc10723c7)

## Version 0.29.0

Creatures move smoothly from pose to pose, with pictures made between the game's own by turning each part at its joint; Camera > Smooth Movement turns it off.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.29.0: creatures move smoothly

[`c292a7f3`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/c292a7f37cb7d02b92ceea2f8c3dd3a23b2680fc)

Between one pose and the next (a walk is four, one every other tick), each
body part's angle is worked out from its joints, the skeleton is rebuilt from
them with the standing foot held, and each part is drawn as its nearest
picture turned about its joint. Turned pictures are made from the player's
own sprites as needed and kept a while. Drawing only; up to two ticks behind.
Carried things, bubbles, the halo, Z's and stars, and a camera following a
creature go with it. Camera &gt; Smooth Movement turns it off.

OPENC2E_WATCH_GLIDE prints jumps from frame to frame; OPENC2E_SNAPSHOTS at
-3,-3 follows a walking creature.

## Version 0.28.5

Z's over creatures for as long as they sleep, and grendel eggs that hatch without the Boney Grendels pack.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.28.5: Z's over sleepers, and grendel eggs without the Boney pack

[`7dada256`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/7dada25687d1075c13e4700bdca27ede521b585f)

A sleeping creature shows the game's own drifting Z (the player's zzzz.s16)
over its head for as long as it sleeps, as C2's stars are shown over an
unconscious one. NEW: GENE uses the standard grendel for "bone" when the
Boney Grendels pack is not there, so those eggs no longer stop mid-hatch.
OPENC2E_SNAPSHOTS at -2,-2 takes a picture at a sleeping creature.

## Version 0.28.4

Spring and summer fruit that feeds: hatchlings no longer starve on a full stomach in a new world.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.28.4: spring and summer fruit that feeds

[`5e88e479`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/5e88e479498ff6d4e91897e23f67ab89cf3fddab)

Hatchlings starved in new worlds (reported by players, 22 November 2026).
The seasonal bushes' fruit is most of what they eat, and the spring and
summer fruits gave only glucose, 60 and 90, where a fruit of the game gives
about 100 starch, each worth 12 glucose to a norn's body: they ate dozens
each, stayed at Hunger 255 and died once their stores ran out, about an
hour old. A new world starts in spring. Both fruits now also give starch
and a little fat and protein, about 1,500 and 1,800 glucose in all, still
the lightest of the four seasons.

Twelve norns hatched at the incubator, a fast game week: 7 of 14 creatures
starved before; none in three runs with this, with 60 to 144 matings and 2
to 9 conceptions each.

## Version 0.28.3

The first players' reports fixed: display scaling on Windows, speaking after naming a world, creatures that grew up unable to breed, sleep broken off within seconds, a crash at some births, and clearer wording of what a creature is doing.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.28.3: norns breed, sleep and hear again; Windows display scaling

[`31d17d7e`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/31d17d7e2421c293e5f6d3b66a8f14d56f5b1d1e)

Fixes for what the first players reported on Reddit (22 November 2026).

- With the desktop scaled (Windows and X11 at 125%, 150%, 200%), the
  interface was laid out for the window's pixels and then drawn larger:
  windows sat off centre or off the screen and the mouse missed what it
  pointed at. It is laid out in the drawn size now, and so is the pointer.
- The first screen always shows the pointer, and its world name box is
  ready to type in.
- Closing a text box stopped typing for the whole window, so nobody could
  speak to the creatures after naming a new world (or anything else) until
  the world was opened again.
- Breeding: a receptor that switches on at a life stage reads its chemical
  at once. One on its own organ's clock was waited for with the clock at 0,
  so the reproductive organ of every creature that grew up since its world
  was opened stopped at adolescence and none could breed. Four fast game
  weeks of twelve hatched norns: no mating at all; with this, 12 to 13
  matings and 1 to 2 conceptions each.
- Sleep: a very sleepy body's own falling asleep (event 69) is a sleep, let
  run; the mind's next choice used to wake it within a second or two, as
  tired as before (710 of 832 sleeps in a test). It does not start over a
  sleep, nor while hunger or water keeps the creature awake. Lying down to
  rest is not learned as sleeping, and lasts until rested. Sleep learned
  before is learned afresh.
- A birth no longer crashes the game when a mutated gene names a breed
  whose pictures are not installed: the creature wears its own species'.
- What a creature is doing is worded better ("dropping a nature").
- Developer aids: OPENC2E_WATCH_SLEEP, OPENC2E_WATCH_SEX.

#### README: the trailer by its YouTube picture

[`6d825577`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/6d82557712d4fe09cb4b5ce435cf0b2af3fd9b96)

The attached video answered only the one who uploaded it (404 signed out).
Also: downloads are no longer rebuilt every night.

#### The nightly release checked: public, or the job fails

[`96694e44`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/96694e44d2f64a48efa46e71d99e3ee4db2d09ba)

GitHub can turn the new release into a draft when the old nightly tag's
deletion is handled late: the job said success and every download was
not found (run 44997355434; published by hand). The job now looks, publishes
a draft again and fails unless a download answers.

#### README: what was tried in Wine

[`fd2d4ded`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/fd2d4dede5d9c265e6ea30c327a61005e422cb85)

## Version 0.28.2

File > Exit saved an emptied world: quitting now saves it whole, an empty world is never saved over a good one, and a world already stripped gets its objects back. File > Exit also works while paused. Players' builds open no listening port, so no firewall question at the first start.

### 22 November 2026

#### Version 0.28.2: File &gt; Exit saves the world whole; no listening port for players

[`030e599f`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/030e599fa9a91a7996327fecb928937d622f92f4)

File &gt; Exit set world.quitting, and the next tick cleared every object and
creature before main() saved the world on quitting: the save it wrote was
empty (0 creatures, 0 objects), and opening the world again removed the
world file's own objects to match. Closing the window was never affected.
The tick no longer clears them (shutdown() does, after the save); Exit now
quits as the window's close button does, so it also works while paused.
An empty world is never written over a save, and a save without a single
object leaves the world file's objects in place, so a world already
stripped gets them back (its creatures are not in the save).
check_saves.py quits each world and checks the save that wrote
(OPENC2E_TEST_QUIT_AFTER_TICKS, developer builds).

The port other programs may send CAOS to is opened in developer builds
only, and on 127.0.0.1 only: a listening program is what makes Windows and
macOS ask about the firewall at the first start.

Found by installing the published build in Wine.

## Version 0.28.0

Faster, and rotten fruit. A world loads in seconds (a crowded one took over a minute at every start in a player's folder), play takes about a third less of the processor, and players' downloads leave out the developer switches; Linux's are fully optimized. Our bushes' fruit now rots where it lies, feeding the soil and the germs of rotting food; eating it hurts and may bring illness, and creatures learn it apart from good fruit. A saved game that cannot be read is now shown on screen, with a way to put it back. Windows builds again (they had failed since 0.19.0).

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.28.0

[`9a94d3fc`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/9a94d3fcbc9412e46557990507ae85ca005f6c61)

#### Rotten fruit: a stronger belly ache

[`0741ea3c`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/0741ea3c1bbe4c310598708330d93bc8ef2e9259)

Pain Increase 60 to 120: at 60 the ache was lost among the other needs that
changed while a creature ate (mean outcome about nothing over 20 tastings).

#### Linux downloads fully optimized

[`e7a20800`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/e7a20800ad4d558054c0d69067860f0fc0405fff)

The Linux 64-bit packages are built with -O3 and link-time optimization: 90
world ticks a second flat out on a copy of Home against 79. (Windows and
macOS stay as they are until their builds have been seen to work.)
check_saves.py no longer fetches breed packs for each check.

#### Starting faster still: picture scaling, and slow-made art kept

[`c62675ea`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/c62675ea3f6c2f9ad943ed35579d8f1680e24e32)

- ImageUtils::Scale works out each sample's source column and row once, not
  for each sample of each pixel (the same pictures); a third of a load went
  there, growing creatures' parts for their life stages.
- Made pictures that take over a tenth of a second (the glass tube's, 2-3 s
  at every start) are kept in the player's own "Zeetures made/Images", and
  made again when the program or the game's own pictures change.
- Home loads in 3.7 s, from 9.6 s this evening.

#### Art tidied: the icon's unused designs, and old paths in comments

[`a9314149`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/a93141495002e7802db4c55c9fdabb14eff18b19)

art/app_icon.py keeps the egg in use (F8) and the upright one before it

#### Rotten fruit

[`b9bc2e6c`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/b9bc2e6c2c8328d877a8410cb5c86f59d0cc31f6)

Our bushes' fruit went ripe, overripe and then vanished, so it fed neither
the soil nor the germs that live in rotting food, as the game's own fruit
does (owner: "given that rotten fruit spreads disease, should we consider
whether the fruits from the plants we made should have a rotten state--and
sprite set?").

- Overripe fruit becomes rotten fruit (2 25 149, pictures zfrr, our own:
  art/rottenfruit.py, from the eight land and sea fruits): moldy, then
  collapsed, for about seven and a half minutes, giving the room one
  organic nutrient in each of five steps (the game's fruit gives five).
  The seedling that sometimes sprouted as it vanished now comes then; its
  long list of places not to sprout is in food.cos once, not four times.
- It smells of nothing and feeds nothing. Eating one hurts the belly at
  once (chemical 18), and may give the illness that lives in rotting food
  (Disease::ateRotten: 0.3 without antibodies).
- The mind sees it as a kind of its own (MIND_ROTTEN_FRUIT), as it does the
  healing fruit, so what eating it does is learned apart; the nearest good
  fruit is still seen when a rotten one lies nearer. The Mind Kit calls the
  two kinds by name (they read as whatever words follow the nouns).
- Four copies of Home, ten minutes flat out, two with the old script: good
  meals 23 and 23 per 1,000 choices (22 and 21 without), mean hungry 12.7
  and 13.6 of 100 (14.3 and 14.6), illnesses caught 231 and 218 (215 and
  218, two of them from rotten fruit), no deaths.

#### Loading ten times faster in a player's folder; a third less CPU in play

[`d485afae`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/d485afae50004efc204465f190847d2b1ebc228b)

Profiled with OPENC2E_SAMPLE on a copy of Home (101 creatures, 60 frames a
second, 1280x720):

- Looking for a file in a folder that is not there went through the whole
  file cache each time to update a folder that could not be updated. A
  player's folder has no Overrides, so a world this size took 65-95 s to
  load at every start; now 9-10 s (and Home, which has the folders, 9.6 to
  6.1 s).
- The window's size was asked of SDL for every sprite of every pass, every
  frame: 13% of play time. Now once a frame.
- The passes drawn a world's width to either side (for the join of the
  world's ends) are made only when the view is near that end, and an
  object's glide is worked out once, not for each pass.
- The creatures' own scents (signatures) were kept in a table by name in
  every room: 7% of play time. Now a column of numbers per creature, the
  same sums.
- The halo and the fainted creatures' stars are looked for ten times a
  second, not at every frame.
- CPU in play: 35% of a core to 24%. Pictures compared before and after at
  14 places (both ends of the world included): the same but for creatures'
  poses.
- The log is written out once a second on every system (it was, on
  Windows) and after the load: it is quiet now, and a file's buffer could
  hold minutes, lost in a crash.

#### Developer switches left out of players' builds; settled old-behavior switches gone

[`31ad4e9d`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/31ad4e9d9c7694bd8784529abc6c5cc93c0d5196)

- src/common/devenv.h: the 294 reads of OPENC2E_ developer switches go
  through devenv(), which is getenv in builds made with OPENC2E_DEV_HOOKS
  (on by default here) and nothing in players' builds (the nightly packages
  now configure with it off), so what they guard is compiled away. A few
  anyone may use stay getenv: OPENC2E_MIND_LOG, \_WINDOW_SIZE, \_WINDOW_POS,
  \_WINDOW_MEMORY, \_STAGE_SCALE, \_STAGE_GROWTH, \_SENILE_TINT.
- Fourteen switches that kept an old behavior "to compare" are gone with
  the old code: OLD_BOAT_FORAGE, \_BOTTOM_LINE, \_DRY_WAY, \_EXPLORE,
  \_RELAY_OUTPUT, \_RESCUE_SIDE, \_SAME_ROOM_SNAP, \_SCRIPT_DOORS, \_SHAFT_PLACE,
  \_SIDE_DROPS, \_STALL_CHECK (with RESTART_METABOLISM), \_STEP_HOLD,
  \_STEP_RISE, \_WALK_DIRECTION, and WALL_LINE.
- The build files' part of removing the never-shipped openc2e tools.
- Unused: the null mind, Exposure::unbornDose, SkeletalCreature::headBox.

#### Never-shipped parts of openc2e removed

#### A message when a saved game had to be set aside

[`4e21d676`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/4e21d6767189255962869982b2b40e7234a79035)

0.27.0 kept a save it could not read but said so only in the log. Now, at
such a load, the world is held still behind "Saved Game Kept Safe", which
names the folder it is kept in, with three choices: Quit and Put It Back
(nothing is saved, and the save is as it was, so another version can open
it; refused if the world has been saved since), Open World Folder, and
Carry On from the original map. A save that could be neither read nor moved
is never overwritten: saving is refused for that session and the message
says so. Tests (WorldSaveTest): kept aside with its contents, put back,
never over a newer save, and nothing made when there is no save.

#### Windows builds again: two things only Unix compilers accepted

[`24da96c2`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/24da96c2cf494470a853803c8fb30fc0008938ac)

Every Windows build has failed since 2e2ca236 (0.19.0), so the nightly
release kept its older Windows files:

- worldStartedFromEden took a std::string and was given a path, which only
  converts by itself where a path's own character is char;
- the recording switch (e5b4ad2e) called popen and pclose, which are
  \_popen and \_pclose there (and write binary only with "wb").

The sound cache's key is the path's UTF-8 form, as path::string() cannot
give every Windows path.

## Version 0.27.0

Lighter and safer: the creature mind no longer writes a running commentary to the log (about 70 MB an hour in a world of a hundred creatures; now a few kilobytes), and each sound is decoded once instead of at every play. (An earlier version of this note also claimed about 100 MB less memory; measured again, the difference was the moment it was measured, not the change.) A save the game cannot read is kept aside rather than overwritten. The downloads carry only the game: no developer tools, and 2.7 MB of unused pictures gone.

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.27.0

[`a204085e`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/a204085ec592c498963de81d699c70ca0da0cc13)

#### Saves: one that cannot be read is kept aside, never overwritten; a check for old saves

[`9b89b7e0`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/9b89b7e09626b037614704d540ae8fc37e5a043b)

Saves have kept the same format line since 2026-11-07 and load keyed
lines with defaults for what an older save lacks, so every version has
loaded the worlds before it (Home has loaded on every one). But a save the
loader could not read (an unknown format line) only printed a note and
started the world from its world file, and the next autosave then
replaced the old save for good; an exception part way through the load
would have stopped the game.

- WorldSave::SetAsideUnreadableSave moves such a save to
  save.unreadable-DATE in the world folder and says so; LoadWorldState
  calls it for an unknown format, and Engine catches a load that throws
  part way and does the same (the world goes on from what loaded).
- scripts/check_saves.py ENGINE C2_FOLDER WORLDS: copies each saved world
  into a throwaway home folder, loads it without autosave, and compares
  "Restored saved world" with the creatures and eggs its save lists. On
  2026-11-21: AtpTest and EatTest (saved 2026-11-08) 8/8 creatures and 21
  eggs each, Home 101/101; a save with its format line changed was set
  aside as save.unreadable-DATE and the world started fresh.

#### Downloads carry only the game; 14 bush pictures nothing plants removed

[`ef2c09b4`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/ef2c09b423cc0d301add9e34f3ac108abe363b62)

- CMakeLists.txt: cmake --install puts in the game, its art and AUTHORS,
  COPYING and README. The developer tools (cobdumper, mngdumper,
  praybuilder, praydumper, spritedumper) and the CAOS reference
  (caosdocs.html, commandinfo.json) are in the devtools component,
  installed only with --component devtools; mngplayer2 only with its own.
  The Windows zip and installer and the Linux tar.gz carried all of them.
- art/sprites: the compact, slender and potted bush forms in looks no
  place uses (zfcm zfcs zfcv zfcw zfnd zfnv zfnw zfod zfos zfow zfqd zfqm
  zfqs zfqv; no version of the additions ever planted them) are gone: 2.7
  MB of every download (art/sprites 8.7 MB to 6.0 MB). zfnm stays: an
  earlier food.cos planted it, so older saves may hold one.
  art/fruitbush.py makes only the forms in SMALL_FORMS_USED.

#### A quieter, lighter game: the mind's commentary off, sounds decoded once

[`12c1432d`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/12c1432da42181adcc2e9eabb4c280029f5eb4eb)

Measured on a copy of Home (101 creatures), the engine wrote 1.4 MB of
output a minute, 94% of it the creature mind's running commentary: about
six lines for every decision of every creature (Home's last log was
280 MB). On Windows that went to zeetures-log.txt one disk write a line,
with no cap.

- HomeostatMind: the 28 commentary prints that had no switch (choices,
  outcomes, stimuli, faces, what is learned, born, died) go through
  MIND_LOG, on only with OPENC2E_MIND_LOG=1; the arguments are not worked
  out when it is off. logChoice no longer copies and sorts the candidates,
  and endEpisode no longer builds its log text, unless something prints
  (the outcome the Mind Kit shows is kept as before). The WATCH_ switches
  print their own lines as before.
- Sounds: each sound file is decoded once and kept (up to 64 MB; a
  Creatures 2 Sounds folder is about 25 MB) instead of reopened and decoded
  at every play (in 150 s of Home the cough alone played 340 times). The
  "Playing" line for each sound only with OPENC2E_WATCH_SOUND.
- The render-target created/destroyed lines are gone; BIG MOVE keeps its
  one line but walks the stack only with OPENC2E_WATCH_MOVES; "Creature
  out of room system", "no down foot room" and the physics-bug lines print
  at most 20 times a run.
- Windows log: buffered (64 KB), written out once a second, and started
  afresh if it passes 20 MB in a session.

Output in 90 s of Home: 2.0 MB before, 23 KB after; memory 565 MB before,
454 MB after. CPU unchanged (about 36% of a core at 1280x720 on the
laptop): the commentary's cost was output, not reckoning.

Developer aids for making the Zeetures trailer (owner, 2026-11-21); they

  frames a second of real time (late frames repeated, early ones

## Version 0.26.0

Creatures poisoned by heavy metals are now cared for like the sick and the young, and creatures learn the volcano's fruit as a medicine of its own: those who know it eases pain bring it to sufferers and go looking for it when they hurt. The Health Kit's Doctor shelf has the volcanic fruit, for heavy metals from radiation poisoning. Swarm bees stuck against walls now join their hive.

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.26.0

[`0e6273fe`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/0e6273fed8d4d1636d09f2d477e5cc6f9918d874)

#### The poisoned are cared for; healing fruit learned apart; Doctor's volcanic fruit

[`480cda63`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/480cda6319eb43d8d0057f21fe92f0d99a3ccbd4)

Owner, 2026-11-21: heavy-metal poisoning should prompt others to bring
the cure as part of caring, and help creatures learn ("care + learned
medicine"); and a volcanic fruit on the Health Kit's Doctor shelf,
"clear it is for heavy metals from radiation poisoning".

- Care: creatures with heavy metals over 50 give off their kind's care
  scent (pheromones.txt "when 140&gt;50") and count as needing care, so
  others tend them (tickle, bring food or a toy).
- Healing fruit apart: the volcanic bushes' fruit (2 25 x, OV02 2) is
  seen by the mind as its own kind, MIND_HEALING_FRUIT (40), past C2's
  categories (to the engine it stays fruit; a choice of it names the
  fruit itself), so what eating it does is learned apart, by the eater
  and by watchers. Habituation widened to 41; thought pictures find it.
- Bringing: a carer tending one in pain brings first what it has learned
  eases pain when eaten (the healing fruit, once known), if in sight.
- The cure trip: in lasting pain, knowing the healing fruit eases it,
  go to the nearest place remembered finding some, for the pain relief.
  Food places now keep their category in the save.
- Doctor shelf: "Volcanic fruit", with a picture and a description that
  opens "For heavy metals from radiation poisoning"; Add to the world
  makes a ripe volcanic fruit beside the creature shown.

Measured, 12-game-day Home copies (care runs dropped heavy metals 21,
34, 42; without the poisoned care scent 30; with and without the
healing kind, four at once, 14/22 against 21/18): no clear speed-up.
Healing fruit was eaten 105-445 times with felt pain relief about -0.55,
carers tended 300-470 times; but most of the poisoned live far from the
volcano and had neither seen the fruit nor watched it eaten, and eaters
learned little relief (the soothing lands as the meal ends), so the cure
trip was rarely offered. Knowledge spreads over many days. Switches
OPENC2E_NO_HEALING_KIND, OPENC2E_NO_CURE_SEEK, OPENC2E_WATCH_CURE.

When a hive dies out it sends a queen and 20 swarm bees (2 17 6) that

## Version 0.25.1

Creatures in lasting pain now go back to places where it eased, and the volcano's fruit soothes the pain of heavy metals as soon as it is eaten, so poisoned creatures find their cure: in a twelve-day test their heavy metals fell almost twice as far, with less pain.

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.25.1

#### Creatures in pain go back to places that eased it; volcanic fruit soothes

Twelve game days in a Home copy (46 creatures with heavy metals over 50)

The mind's favourite places now include pain, from a need of 0.25 (a

## Version 0.25.0

Heavy metals now matter: a creature carrying them feels a dull pain that grows with the amount, and they slowly wear down one of its organs until, left for days, they can kill it; the volcano's fruit cures them. Radiation also lowers the chance of conceiving, and a baby exposed to it in its mother or its egg hatches with extra mutations, which its own children inherit.

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.25.0

[`2da71d25`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/2da71d255cf65c4d73ddf7827f152516850bf309)

#### Heavy metals hurt; radiation lowers fertility and mutates the unborn

[`d321e4f4`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/d321e4f460238dbd3a2a3af84333097a7099580b)

Owner, 2026-11-21: make heavy metals matter, "slow but serious, as
pain", and radiation should affect fertility and mutation in offspring
in utero and in the egg. Exposure.cpp, our own design:

- Heavy metals (140) above 16 are felt as pain (chemical 1, 1/10 a
  heavy metal every 10 ticks) and wear the organ the genome marks for
  them (its heavy-metals receptor) in proportion. A norn held at 255:
  pain about 145, the organ down to 9% in about 4.7 game days; at 120,
  pain about 60. EDTA from volcanic fruit cures it.
- Radiation dose: radiation above 10 a creature stands in adds to its
  dose (radiation-minutes), halving in a game day; the chance to
  conceive is divided by 1 + dose/1000 for each partner.
- Before birth: a baby's genome gathers the radiation above 10 around
  it, in its mother and then its egg; at hatching each 1500 of dose is
  one more mutation (at most 10), written to its genome file so its
  children inherit them (Breeding.cpp mutatedCopy). Eggs at the
  volcano's mouth (radiation 56) gain about one a game day.

Doses are saved with the world. Switches OPENC2E_NO_EXPOSURE,
OPENC2E_WATCH_EXPOSURE, OPENC2E_EXPOSURE_MUTATION_DOSE (tests).

## Version 0.24.0

Radiation spreads out from the volcano and the sky as in the original game, following doors as they open and close. The fruit of the volcano's ember-red bushes now carries EDTA, the only cure for the heavy metals that radiation builds up in a creature.

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.24.0

[`1cd1e5b2`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/1cd1e5b2b03630166ad6f88a9db43f3be11b9852)

#### Radiation spreads from its sources; volcanic fruit gives EDTA

[`6736654d`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/6736654d4b30ee5a38203aa13cabde8e69f1fb40)

Rooms kept the world file's radiation whatever opened or closed.
World::tickRadiation, every 10 ticks: radiation passes through doorways
(1/8 of the difference at full openness, as heat), a source adds 0.35 x
RSRC, and every room loses a tenth. Fitted to the world file's values
(a snapshot of the real game's: the volcano's chamber 45-179 from
sources 20-100, the sky rooms 17-55 from sources 5-30): the rule's
steady state explains 85% of them. Over five minutes of a Home copy:
55 rooms above 5 (66 before), the volcano's peak 185 (179); creatures'
heavy metals hardly changed (median 46, 49 with the old fixed values).
Switch OPENC2E_NO_RADIATION_SPREAD.

Heavy metals (140), made from radiation over 48 by every standard
genome, barely decay (half-life 248), and EDTA (157), which nothing in
the world gave, is all that removes them; it does nothing else. The
fruit of the volcanic bushes now gives EDTA, 60 ripe and 30 overripe
(owner): one ripe fruit took a norn's heavy metals from 222 to 188,
an uneating norn's stayed at 218.

## Version 0.23.0

Air pressure moves between rooms and makes the wind, as in the original game: storms raise it, the weather machines push it up or down, and the wind blows from high pressure to low, so the clouds drift with the weather instead of always meeting the same wind. The Ecology Kit's pressure and wind maps show it change.

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.23.0

[`aa0130fc`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/aa0130fc4a8a472f308b9f167fe3d86c83387abf)

#### Air pressure spreads and makes the wind

[`ccdfd1fe`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/ccdfd1fe0c00e88c9cbdd3ab06c9dad4d6cb4de8)

Rooms kept the world file's pressure and wind for good, so the storms'
"addv pres 100" and the weather machines' pressure sources did nothing
(the storms' pressure only piled up, past 500), and the clouds, which
steer by WNDX/WNDY, always met the same wind. World::tickPressure, every
10 ticks: pressure passes through doorways (1/32 of the difference at
full openness), a pressure source adds PSRC/20, and each room settles
toward the world file's pressure. The wind is made from the differences,
as in the real game: the world file's wind (a snapshot of the real
game's) blows from high toward low pressure in 209 of 216 rooms and
fits the sum over doorways of the pressure difference times the
doorway's openness along the line between the rooms' middles; scaled so
its strength matches the snapshot (rooms' median about 17, 90th
percentile about 50), with gusts near storms. Clears five rooms' junk
wind values. Switch OPENC2E_NO_WIND. The Ecology Kit says pressure and
wind now change. The room dump (OPENC2E_DUMP_ROOMS) also prints the
sources and the wind.

## Version 0.22.0

Bacteria and disease, as in the original game: germs live in dark caves and rotting food and are carried by grendels; creatures catch them, sneeze, cough and run a fever, spread them by sneezing, and get better as their antibodies build up. Others look after the ill as they do the young. The Health Kit shows a creature's illness, and the Ecology Kit's map shows where the germs are. Each kit can now turn its own notices on the status bar on or off.

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.22.0

[`0e4cbfc1`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/0e4cbfc1b92b8c22c42cd6385a26e685d1f2dae1)

#### Bacteria and disease; notice switches in each kit

[`3231e3fa`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/3231e3fa4de9efc1bd0d60718d07b3a2c92a1b99)

C2's germs (the manual: bacteria in caves and carried by grendels;
coughing, sneezing and fever; sneezing spreads it) were missing: no
world script makes antigens, and SNEZ was a stub. The genomes already
carry the body's side (antigens wear organs, the immune organ turns them
into antibodies and fever toxin, histamine sets off sneezing and
coughing). Disease.cpp adds the germs, our own design: strains with an
antigen and a toxin; germs per room (dark caves keep them, rotting food
feeds them, light kills them, they seep through doorways); grendels
carry a strain for life without falling ill; a creature may catch a
strain unless it has its antibody; while ill the germs feed antigen and
toxin into it, and it is over when the antibody has built up; SNEZ
passes illnesses to creatures in front; now and then a strain changes
its antigen. Saved with the world. Tuned as a nuisance (owner): in
eight-minute Home copies each creature fell ill 1-4 times in about four
game hours, no deaths from illness. Switches OPENC2E_NO_DISEASE,
OPENC2E_WATCH_DISEASE.

The ill give off their kind's care scent and are tended like the young
(pheromones.txt "when ill"). The Health Kit's Fitness page shows the
illness and its antibodies; the Ecology Kit's map shows germs by strain
with the ill ringed; "Ill" and "Recovered" notices; a death while ill
is recorded as illness.

Each kit has checkboxes for its own status-bar notices (kept in
kits.ini): births, pregnancies and eggs in the Breeder's Kit, deaths in
the Graveyard Kit, illness, recovery, fainting and coming round in the
Health Kit, changes in the Owner's Kit. A notice switched off is still
in the life log.

## Version 0.21.2

A creature that starves is recorded as starved, not as "organ failure", even when it fainted before its hunger had risen.

### 21 November 2026

#### Version 0.21.2

[`8b899577`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/8b8995779bc36430ee68bd85d9f832480382896c)

#### A creature that starved with Hunger frozen low is recorded as starved

[`9c9e0953`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/9c9e0953310121d44dfd19635849c883ff1a3dc4)

Once ATP is gone a C2 creature's chemistry stands still, Hunger with it,
so a starving creature could faint and die with the Hunger it had when
it crashed (about 100) and a little glucose it could no longer burn, and
was recorded as dying of "organ failure". Three Home hatchlings (5RYI,
6DOB, 1RIS) were; their genomes hatched again in a copy starved the
same way, with glycogen, fat and adipose tissue all at 0. A death with
those three stores spent now counts as starvation.

The submarine no longer falls off the screen when a game is loaded. Creatures can no longer push it while it travels: that restarted its trip part-way, and over many trips it lost track of its own settings.

### 21 November 2026

In C1/C2 a creature's activate 1 or 2 on an object that is not
activateable (ATTR 4) is now refused, as the hand's click already is
(switch OPENC2E_ACTIVATE_ANYTHING; OPENC2E_WATCH_REFUSED logs refusals).
The submarine clears the bit for a trip; a creature's push part-way
restarted its trip script, whose "subv attr 4" then ran again with no
"addv attr 4" to match. In Home its ATTR had run down to -68 (every bit
set, physics among them) and the airlock doors' to -60, so at launch it
fell straight down through the world.

At load the submarine's ATTR is set to its docked value from the game's
Eden world (12) and the airlock doors' to 4; its callers now act only
when it is activateable again, i.e. when its trip script has finished.

## Version 0.21.0

Creatures learn by watching each other, from their own family most and when young most: what things are good for, where food is, and which places and drops hurt. They name the things they use, often for a young one of their family, and family pick up each other's words faster.

### 20 November 2026

#### Version 0.21.0

[`db545f0c`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/db545f0c19a1c064be6550ae8bb29b25db8675f5)

#### Creatures learn by watching others, family most

[`ec9f8f09`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/ec9f8f09401e738b523777faf6f736f43f75f1f1)

Owner, 2026-11-20 (inheritance talk, "learning from parents"): family
most, the young most, about a third as strong as doing it oneself; what
things do, where food is, danger, and words.

Each mind posts what it has just done to a shared board (not saved): the
outcome of using a thing (what each felt drive did), a meal and where,
being hurt in a room (pain, injury, fear, suffocation risen 0.04 over 10
ticks), a painful fall and where it stepped off. Every mind reads the
posts since its last tick and takes in those from creatures it can see,
at weight watchShare (1/3) x kin 1 / others 0.35 x its life stage (1, 1,
0.8, 0.6, 0.45, 0.35, 0.25): outcomes into its expectation for that thing
and action (its own readiness genes apply), meals as a remembered food
place (confidence up to 0.6), hurt as the room's feel, falls as a painful
drop. Kin are close kin (Breeding.h closeKin), now marked on each seen
creature (SeenAgent::kin, MindPerception::self).

Words: a child or older that has just used a thing names it now and then
("look fruit"; meaning name:C, C2's noun 16 + C), half the time when a
baby or child of its family is in sight; family hear each other's words
twice as strongly. Switch OPENC2E_NO_WATCHING; OPENC2E_WATCH_WATCHING
logs each thing learned by watching.

Three matched pairs of 8-minute copies of Home: at Hunger 250+ 22.4% vs
25.0%, deaths 11.7 vs 12.3 a copy, words learned 144 vs 105 (fruit 57,
button 38 and lift 37 vs 0, 0 and 9); creatures spoke 36% more. ASan run
clean (only the old brain's leaks at exit).

## Version 0.20.4

An object that came to rest half inside the rock is set back where it fits.

### 20 November 2026

#### Version 0.20.4

[`2bc272b1`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/2bc272b1df1d7f5b1d4c72d6c9780966aa474fa7)

#### Objects resting half in the rock are set back inside

[`757e3b1d`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/757e3b1d6a7232ee1692b651ecd1b84106c8e58b)

C2 physics looks for the rock only while an object moves, from the
middle of each side of its box, so an object that came to rest partly in
the rock was never looked at again. A survey of every object's box edges
in a copy of Home (15 samples over 5 minutes, 15,332 sightings) found one:
a 2 15 7 at 4550,1584 by the dome's dock bay, its left, right and top in
the rock over room 38, there in every sample. Now and then a resting
object checks those four points; one in no room at all is handed to
healOutOfRoomSystem, which sets it down where it fits. Objects that only
sit across a wall between two rooms (call buttons, plants) are left alone.
Switch OPENC2E_NO_ROCK_CHECK.

Rerun: that object was found once and set on the bay's floor (1518 top);
nothing else was flagged.

The survey also settles the corner-only collisions worry: apart from
that object, no object had an edge in the rock away from the world's
seam, and no big object straddled thin rock, so the four-point check
stays as C2 has it.

## Version 0.20.0

Genes shape the mind: part of each creature's temperament comes from its own genes' chemistry (bred and custom lines can be bolder, calmer or quicker to anger), and eight mind settings are inherited: how fast it learns, how decisive and patient it is, how much it wanders, and how readily it learns about danger, food, water and company. Shown in the Owner's Kit; genes.csv tracks the population's averages over generations.

### 20 November 2026

#### Version 0.20.0

[`3fbb1b63`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/3fbb1b63efd159a30b8e046e5a01ece9cb127cfd)

#### Genes shape the mind: temperament from C2 genes, mind genes, readinesses

[`996529f1`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/996529f178ddb3710e9eba4e0d2f0e9c1f5ea921)

Owner's choices (2026-11-20), all three of the remaining gaps:

Temperament from the creature's own C2 genes (GenomeTraits): each drive's
chemistry against its species' standard genome (norn, gren, ettn.gen):
how much its stimulus genes raise the drive (chemical 18 + drive), how
much its emitters make its chemicals, its chemical's half-life (8 steps
double it) and its receptors' gain on the drive locus, as summed log
ratios. Traits move by 0.7 times those gains (at most 1.5): fear against
boldness, boredom curiosity, tiredness and sleepiness against energy,
loneliness (less half of crowding) sociability, anger temper, stress
reactivity. The stock eggs read zero; of 1,721 genomes on disk 282
differ (family lines: +0.79 temper, -0.53 sociability; Barbie and Sting
bold and hot-tempered, Grendenile shy). temperament = born + drift +
this; children take after born + drift (the genes go with the genome).
OPENC2E_DUMP_GENE_TRAITS lists every genome; OPENC2E_NO_GENOME_TRAITS.

Mind genes (MindGenes): learning, decisiveness, patience, wanderlust,
and readiness to learn about danger, food, water and company (the
Baldwin effect: nothing learned is passed on). Inherited as temperament
(0.4 of the parents' average plus chance) in Genetics/NAME.mind, drawn
for the game's eggs, saved with the world, kept by the Genetics tidy.
They scale learningRate, temperature, travelHalf, exploreWant and, per
drive, the learning rate (driveLearning). genes.csv beside census.csv
keeps the population's averages every census. OPENC2E_NO_MIND_GENES.

Owner's Kit, Temperament: what its genes' chemistry gives, and the eight
mind genes as bars. Fixed: the younger-stage rebuild (Creatures menu,
SetCreatureLifeStage) drew a new temperament, rhythm, pace of ageing
and mind genes; OPENC2E_MIND_PARAMS was lost when temperament changed.

Tests (copies of Home, 3 each, 8 minutes, on vs off): at Hunger 255
22.8% vs 22.7%, deaths 2 vs 2, conceptions wrote .mind files, hatchlings
log their gene shifts; 150 of 150 creatures keep every inherited value
across a save and load.

## Version 0.19.1

Creatures explore more, and more wisely: a place stays interesting until visited a few times and becomes interesting again after a long while away, and the young explore most.

### 20 November 2026

#### Version 0.19.1

[`565e665d`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/565e665dbbfc983d2fc9c8c6ba219a5183d26c24)

#### Mind: exploring by novelty that fades with visits and returns with time

[`ecc7b81f`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/ecc7b81fec7d34fba0c40d82b0f21464f0b580da)

Owner's choices (2026-11-20): counts fade, novelty returns, the young
explore most; twice the old pull. A room's novelty is 1 / sqrt(1 +
visits), its visits fading with time away (by e in 52,000 ticks), so
rarely visited rooms keep drawing a bored creature and old haunts become
worth a look again (count-based exploration: Strehl and Littman 2008,
Bellemare et al. 2016; Berlyne 1960). Before, a room's pull ended at its
first visit. The explore trip goes to the room next door or the known
room within reach on this floor most novel for its distance (not the
room it is in; none under novelty 0.25), its want times that novelty.
Each life stage weighs it (baby 1, child 1.5, adolescent 1.35, youth
1.15, adult 1, old 0.8, senile 0.6; Gopnik 2020).

Tests (copies of Home, 3 each, 8 minutes): explore choices per 1,000
decisions 4.5 (old) / 5.2 (1x) / 12.7 (2x) / 34 (4x); places covered
16-19 map cells in all; at maximum Hunger 16.4% / 15.7% / 17.3% / 17.2%;
deaths 1 a copy. OPENC2E_OLD_EXPLORE restores the old rule and pull;
OPENC2E_MIND_PARAMS takes exploreWant, noveltyMin, noveltyReturn.

A COB that cannot be added says why instead of closing the game. Custom worlds (not made from Eden) no longer get the additions written for Eden's map.

The world additions are written for the game's own world. On a custom

or changed rooms it does not have. They now run only when the world was
started from Eden.sfc (its world.sfc the same file); six that change

#### C2: old COBs run as they did in Creatures 2

[`9ffbde82`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/9ffbde82217c94c36c465424171058dfe9605549)

The owner's archive of old COBs (2026-11-20), each imported and added by
hand in a copy of Home: 71 of 109 objects worked before; 115 of 116 now
(the last lacks a sprite its zip never had, and says so).

Commands: SAY$ (the lexer takes "$" in words) and SAY# speak (SAY# also
teaches the word to listeners); INS#; LCUS as a value; NEW: SCEN makes a
scenery object (marked, saved with the world, as the world file's are)
and ESCN enumerates them; WIND and SETV CLAS in C2 as in C1; species
above 65535 wrap to 16 bits, as C2 files its scripts (Zora Castle).

Blocks: the original engines ran DOIF, ELSE, ENDI, NEXT as they came, and
COBs rely on it (NEXT inside DOIF, a second ELSE, DOIF open at ENDM: the
Holo Doc, Coconut tree update, Ball Update, Flower Pack sunflower, the
Mystic Gong, Raining Monarch Butterfly). A C1/C2 statement that will not
compile nested is compiled again, flat, to its script's end
(caosScript::compileFlat; ops FLATENUM and FLATNEXT, a loop stack in the
VM); a command cut short by ENDM is left out (The Albian Sea Bay's own
scripts). Unit tests: c2_flat_else_twice, c2_flat_doif_open_at_endm,
c2_flat_next_in_doif.

Values: in C1/C2 an agent orders against a number by its UNID ("doif targ
gt 0"), and a number stands for the agent with that UNID where an agent
is wanted. MATE with no creature in attention does nothing. A C2 part
whose first picture is past its file's end shows the last (as poses do).

## Version 0.18.4

Creatures no longer fall through the upper-east deck over the lift's shaft, or out of the lift's cage at its top stop: a floor line running along a room's bottom now holds creatures and objects too big for it.

### 20 November 2026

#### Version 0.18.4

[`c7ce359a`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/c7ce359a6e3034878fa830dad36f1755df80c572)

#### C2: a floor line along a room's bottom holds what it is solid for

[`6d063955`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/6d06395525e7e623579cfefb0163ec148f228359)

The physics check for a room's floor line looked only at the room an
agent had moved into, so where the line runs along the room's own
bottom over an open door (255) a fall crossed into the room below
before the line was looked at. Creatures put or stepping on the
upper-east deck over the lift's shaft (room 143, line at its bottom,
floor value 50) fell 213 px to the deck below, and one let go in the
lift's cage at its top stop fell out of it (owner's screencast,
2026-11-13). Falling out of a C2 room's bottom where its floor line
is within 2 px of that bottom, at a size above the floor value and
from above the line, is now a collision: the agent lands on the line.
Rooms touched: the lines' ends at the walkways (65, 66, 192, 227,
233), the treehouse platform (59) and the shaft's top (143).
OPENC2E_OLD_BOTTOM_LINE restores the old check.

Tests (copies of Home): norns dropped every 20 px across the shaft's
top all land on the deck (before: 5 of 6 over the shaft fell); a norn
in the cage at the top stop stands; riders stay in the cage the whole
way up; 4-minute copies old/new: 30/29 falls, 1/1 deaths.

## Version 0.18.3

Each creature ages at its own pace, 0.9 to 1.1 times its genome's lifespan, drawn at hatching: creatures of one genome no longer all die at the same age, and a hatching boom no longer becomes a wave of old-age deaths.

### 20 November 2026

#### Version 0.18.3

[`5ad16c0e`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/5ad16c0e0684693fffda75cbb5423c49165707e2)

#### Each creature ages at its own pace

[`d55e606d`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/d55e606d5795ceacf9d1af8ec6562db37ea981eb)

A creature's lifespan was exactly its genome's: the Life chemical (76)
decays by the genome's half-life gene, so every creature of one genome
died at the same age (all the world's ettins; a hatching boom's norns,
in a wave of old-age deaths about 10 hours later). Each C2 creature now
draws its own pace at hatching, 0.9-1.1 (the sum of two draws, most near
1): its Life decay steps fall due 1/pace times as often, so it lives
about pace times its genome's lifespan. C2's lifespans stay the default.
Saved as lifespan-scale; older saves draw one at load.
OPENC2E_NO_LIFE_SPREAD keeps every creature at its genome's.

Test (copy of Home, two saves 60,000 ticks apart): fast agers (mean
0.95) lost Life 5.2% faster than the middle and slow ones (1.05) 4.2%
slower; all 124 creatures in both saves kept their pace.

A starving creature with no food in sight and no known way out tries a lift: it rides one that is there, calls one, or walks to one it can see (the west airlock's corridor, whose only ways out are lifts).

#### Homeostat mind: a starving creature with no way out rides a lift

[`24c647df`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/24c647df98b28cb06bfdc5cb7bccbff69d896d3c)

Owner, 2026-11-20 ("smarter lift use", for the west airlock's corridor,
rooms 120-118, whose only ways out are two cage lifts). Starving creatures
there stayed for minutes at Hunger 255: of 412 lift actions in the
corridor in two copies only 27 led to a ride; their food memories were
thousands of px off, worth too little for the floor-change steps, and the
starve-leave rule left lifts out. Now starve-leave, as its last resort
after known far links and teleporters: ride a lift that is here (a way not
yet found futile; activate 1 and 2 go opposite ways), or press its call
button, or walk to a lift in sight on this floor. Rides are learned as
any other (learnRide). OPENC2E_NO_STARVE_LIFT turns it off.

Tests. Eight norns put in the corridor, 5 minutes: 7 and 7 left (median
exit 40 and 140 s) vs 5 and 7 with the switch off (120 and 110 s). Whole
world, matched copies: hungry 36% and 36% vs 36% and 35%, starving 24%
and 20% vs 22% and 24%: no change. The corridor's own crowd did not fall
(about 4 vs 0.6-3.7): it has a berry bush (x 150-200) that creatures
remember and come to, by lift too.

#### Nightly: the macOS build script executable; the AppImage check passes

[`1950816f`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/1950816f2baa8025fd0781393a0719ccfd2c0ad0)

The first run of the new packaging (2026-11-20) failed in four jobs:
packaging/macos/build.sh was committed without its executable bit (both
macOS jobs: "Permission denied"), and both AppImages were made but their
check ran --version, which exits 1 in openc2e, so the Linux jobs failed
and the release was skipped. The check now looks for the program's
output instead. (The Ubuntu 22.04 build with GCC 12 compiled and ran.)

## Version 0.18.0

Install and play with no command line: a Windows installer, a Mac app in a disk image (Apple Silicon and Intel) and Linux AppImages. Zeetures finds Creatures 2 in the usual Steam and GOG folders, or asks for its folder once and remembers it (File > Creatures 2 Folder changes it). The nightly keeps a system's download from the night before when its build fails, so macOS no longer drops out.

### 20 November 2026

#### Version 0.18.0

[`2d1670d4`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/2d1670d4db9c760d603988e40b06fb4d6e90e848)

#### README: Download and play without a command line

[`b5efed89`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/b5efed89f4d069b00ac1a60de2c972d583336b4b)

Which file to download for each system (Zeetures-Setup.exe,
Zeetures-macOS.dmg, the AppImages) and the one-time steps: SmartScreen's
"Run anyway" for the unsigned installer, Privacy &amp; Security's "Open
Anyway" for the unsigned Mac app (right-click, Open on macOS 14 and
earlier), marking the AppImage executable. How Zeetures finds Creatures
2 (Steam libraries, GOG, ~/Games on a Mac), the first-run folder window,
File &gt; Creatures 2 Folder, game-folder.txt and Windows's
zeetures-log.txt; -d is now optional. The On a Mac section runs the app
instead of a command, and the archives' README-NIGHTLY.txt says the same.

(Part of the section was already committed with 0.17.5.)

#### Nightly: an installer, a Mac app and AppImages; macOS no longer drops out

[`05f32d3f`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/05f32d3ff8c668de2f3658a24c3fda86ef9e9011)

Owner, 2026-11-20: a package players just install and start. The
nightly release now carries, beside the plain archives:

- Zeetures-Setup.exe (packaging/windows/zeetures.iss, Inno Setup, built
  by the new windows-installer job from the 64- and 32-bit builds): one
  installer for both, installing the 64-bit program on 64-bit Windows
  (and Windows on Arm). It installs for the current user with no
  administrator prompt (all users is offered), adds Start-menu and
  desktop shortcuts and an uninstaller that leaves the player's worlds,
  shows a notice page (bring your own Creatures 2, LGPL 2.1, the source
  link), and can start Zeetures at the end.
- Zeetures-macOS.dmg (packaging/macos/build.sh and make-dmg.sh): one
  universal program (arm64 and x86_64, macOS 10.15 and later) as
  Zeetures.app, the art in Contents/Resources (SDL_GetBasePath's folder
  inside an app), the license and readme inside, signed ad hoc; the disk
  image holds the app, a shortcut to Applications, a Read me (allowing
  an unsigned app once) and the license. It replaces the two macOS zips.
- Zeetures-x86_64.AppImage (packaging/linux/make-appimage.sh), built by
  the new linux-appimage job on Ubuntu 22.04 with GCC 12 and a static
  libstdc++, so it runs on systems as old as that (glibc 2.35); the
  bundled SDL3 is static, so the program needs only libc, libz and what
  SDL loads at run time. The AppImage runtime is the static type-2 one
  (no libfuse2 needed). The linux job builds one on 24.04 as well, used
  only if the 22.04 one fails. Zeetures-i686.AppImage from linux-32.

macOS: the arm64 and Intel jobs failed for want of a runner on
2026-11-19 and 11-20 ("The job was not acquired by Runner"), leaving the
release without them. Now one job (macos-15) builds the universal app,
and if it does not finish, macos-fallback builds the same on an Intel
runner (macos-15-intel), a separate pool. The release job also keeps
any of the downloads whose build failed from the previous nightly, and
the release notes say which and why, so no system drops out.

The release notes say which file to pick for each system. On another
branch the workflow builds everything and publishes nothing.

#### Zeetures finds the player's Creatures 2 folder by itself, or asks once

[`c6afb27e`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/c6afb27ec6fa5be3de61e982ea1794c3b7d0df2d)

Owner, 2026-11-20: players should only have to start the program, with no
command line. Before, Linux and macOS needed -d (without it the program
looked for ./data and quit with a message only a terminal shows), and
Windows opened openc2e's bare folder picker on every start, remembering
nothing.

GameFolder.cpp: without -d, the folder remembered from an earlier run
(game-folder.txt in the user folder above the per-game ones:
~/.openc2e, Documents\My Games, ~/Documents/openc2e Data); else ./data if
it holds a game, as in openc2e; else the usual places: every Steam
library (each Steam root, including Debian's ~/.steam/debian-installation,
the Flatpak and Snap ones, and the Windows registry's SteamPath, plus the
libraries its libraryfolders.vdf lists), GOG (the Windows registry's
GOG.com\Games paths, C:\GOG Games, GOG Galaxy\Games, Program Files),
~/Games and Wine's drive_c on Linux, and on a Mac ~/Games/Creatures The
Albian Years and the folder Steam's download_depot fills. What is found
is remembered. Either the Creatures 2 folder or the Creatures The Albian
Years folder that holds it is accepted.

If none is found, a first-run window (SDL3 message box) says Zeetures
plays from the player's own copy and offers Choose Folder..., How to Get
It (the README's Download and play section) and Quit; Choose Folder opens
the system's folder chooser (SDL3, starting in Steam's games folder) and
checks the choice (Creatures 1's folder or another folder gets a message
and the chooser again). File &gt; Creatures 2 Folder... (in the toolbar
since 0.17.5's commit) chooses another folder for the next start; its
tooltip shows the current one. -d still comes first and is not
remembered.

main.cpp: a fatal error at startup is also shown in a message box when
there is no terminal (started from a file manager, the Start menu or the
Dock); SDL_SetAppMetadata names the program Zeetures
(io.github.dotairzee.zeetures, the AppImage's desktop entry). The window
is titled Zeetures for Creatures 2 from the start.

Windows: the program is Zeetures.exe, a windowed program (no console
window behind the game; /ENTRY:mainCRTStartup keeps main), with the egg
icon (zeetures.rc). Started from a command prompt it writes there;
otherwise its output goes to zeetures-log.txt in Documents\My Games.

packaging/icons: zeetures.png, .ico and .icns, the window icon's egg
(openc2e's 2008 logo) scaled up, for Windows, macOS and Linux.

## Version 0.17.5

The Genetics folder is tidied when a world loads: babies' genomes that no world needs any more go to the system's Trash, so the folder no longer grows by hundreds of files a day.

### 20 November 2026

#### Version 0.17.5

[`e26c1a31`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/e26c1a31a3047498fb903e9d2d3854aab4f87122)

#### Tidy the Genetics folder at world load: unused generated genomes to the Trash

[`fc5c2185`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/fc5c2185a5d0b34c574740404afed9bd3b69997b)

Owner, 2026-11-20 ("tidy at world load"). Every conception, NEW: GENE and
splice writes a generated genome (z and three letters, with .season and
.temperament beside it) to the player's Genetics folder; most are needed
only until the baby hatches. Home's folder had 8,527 of them (12,529
files, 307 MB), 7,084 named by no world's save. After a saved world is
restored, tidyGeneratedGenomes reads every world's .txt files for the
generated names they mention and moves each generated genome file that
none mentions, and that is over a day old, to the system Trash: the
freedesktop.org Trash on Linux (files/ and info/\*.trashinfo), ~/.Trash on
macOS, the Recycle Bin on Windows (SHFileOperation with undo). Only
generated names in the user data folder's Genetics; nothing if no save
could be read; OPENC2E_NO_GENOME_TIDY turns it off. Departed donors'
genes are kept in the world folder, not here. Test (scratch copy of the
owner's folder and Home): 10,300 files to the scratch Trash in 0.9 s,
1,456 named kept, every genome Home's save names still present.

## Version 0.17.0

The Breeder's Kit has an Insemination tab for female creatures: choose any creature of her kind, male or female, living, departed or a genome file, see the two side by side with their temperaments and kinship, and she conceives from its genes. Creatures' genes are kept when they die so they can be chosen later.

### 20 November 2026

kind's genes, male or female, to impregnate her (the owner's chosen

donor picker over living creatures, departed ones and genome files; both
side by side with the Owner's Kit temperament bars; kinship from the
family records; her readiness (adolescent or older, not pregnant, not set
infertile, genes readable); Inseminate.

Breeding: inseminate() crosses her genome with the donor's as a mating
does, passes on temperament and seasonal rhythm (drawn at random for a
donor without them), sets BABY and her pregnancy; her life story says

egg". A creature's genes are now kept when it dies (the world folder's
departed/MONIKER.gen and .traits) so departed creatures can be donors.
Genome files: the player's and the game's Genetics folders, without the
generated z names. TraitBar is shared from the Owner's Kit.

Developer aids: OPENC2E_TEST_SELECT=female, OPENC2E_TEST_INSEM="SOURCE\[:NAME\]",
OPENC2E_TEST_INSEM_FRAMES. Tests (copies of Home): living donor, genome
file and departed donor each conceived; the file donor's baby was laid
and hatched in the run; the tab is hidden for a male.

#### The Metamorph Device in the gene splicer room

[`7f08dbcc`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/7f08dbcc2a8c98cbe8bc63a8451c02d1240a71cb)

The Life Kit's Metamorph Device is built into every world from the
player's own COB (builtin-cobs.txt) and moved by metamorph.cos to the gene
splicer room's floor, between the teleporter and the tall threaded pillar
(owner, 2026-11-19, from the mock-up metamorph-lab-mockup.png). Our own
script for it: a creature in its cabin is replaced by a newly hatched copy
of the opposite sex from its own genome; OV10 picks how the creature put in
ends (0 at once, the default; 1 the COB's five poisons, let out to die), the
two versions the COB has. Only the hand runs it (creatures press things
often, and it ends the creature inside); its run cannot be cut off.

Tested in Home copies: the hand's click on the button ran it; a male norn
in the cabin gave a female copy (NEW: GENE of one creature) that hatched
beside the device, in both versions (the poisoned norn fell unconscious
within the test).

#### C2 hotspots set by KNOB run the right script

[`e6d9e1db`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/e6d9e1db3281b02b65ed5606926e72fbcfe106fc)

CompoundAgent::setHotspotFunc stored a hotspot function's message already
turned into a script number, and handleClick turned it into one again: a
hand's click on a hotspot set by KNOB ran activate 2 instead of activate 1
(and deactivate instead of activate 2). Objects from the world file were
unaffected (their message numbers are read raw), but objects made by
scripts were: COB devices such as the Metamorph Device, and the gene lab's
announcement window. The message number is stored raw now, as KMSG does.

Found with the Metamorph Device: a click on its button fired activate 2
("no script"); now activate 1, and the device runs.

## Version 0.14.1

Fixes: the gene splicer works: two creatures in its pods give an egg crossed from both (one gives a clone), and the Owner's Kit says the baby was spliced from them; eggs being hatched can no longer be cut off by a creature pushing, pulling or hitting them, which left grendel, ettin and norn eggs that never hatched, and eggs stuck that way start again at load.

### 19 November 2026

#### Version 0.14.1

[`58cd1380`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/58cd1380728f282f9e2a59b0e519c39d70dc0666)

#### Eggs: a hatching cannot be cut off

[`3554c069`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/3554c06954b0b182fffa50b10745b46570a66439)

Once an egg starts hatching its timer is stopped, and a creature pushing,
pulling, hitting or trying to eat it runs the world's catch-all object
script (2 0 0 1-3, 12) on the egg, which ended the hatching midway with the
timer still stopped. The egg then never hatched: 13 of the 22 grendel and
ettin eggs at the gene lab's chute in Home were stuck that way, one since
2026-11-08.

- The egg machine's and the egg pile's eggs (grendels.cos) and the norn
  egg (script-patches.txt) LOCK their hatching once it starts, with a retry
  timer in case it is ever cut off.
- At load, grendel and ettin eggs stuck mid-hatch are started again (they
  hatch as the population limit allows); empty shells are cleared.

Test, every egg poked once a second as a creature would: old scripts, 12
machine eggs stuck for good; new, all 12 hatched.

#### C2: the gene splicer makes its egg

[`65940b09`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/65940b0900acbb84976617512c74bec9ff4cab13)

The gene lab's splicer takes the creatures in its two pods (killing them),
then crosses their genomes with NEW: GENE. NEW: GENE found a creature's
genome only through the living creature, so it failed ("failed to find
genome") once the pods had taken them: both creatures were lost, no egg was
made, and the console's script died partway. It also refused a 0 parent,
which the console passes with only one pod filled.

- The genomes of the last 64 creatures taken out of the world are kept by
  moniker (historyManager::goneGenomes, filled in Agent::kill), and NEW:
  GENE looks there after the living creatures.
- NEW: GENE with one parent 0 copies the other (a clone, as the
  Metamorphosis device makes).
- A creature hatched from a splice of two creatures is recorded as
  spliced (history event 1, not conceived), saved as "parents M F
  spliced", and the Owner's Kit says "spliced from M and F in the gene
  lab".

Tested in Home copies: two norns in the pods gave an egg (genome crossed
from both) that hatched a baby; one norn gave a clone; the record survives
a save and a reload; the ASan build was clean.

## Version 0.14.0

Creatures look after their friends: food a creature carries over and drops counts as its gift, a well-fed creature brings food to a hungry friend, and creatures may go after one that just hurt a friend; foragers no longer row straight back to a shore they just searched; railings close the treehouse platform's and the scaffold's open ends; creatures no longer step off into the cage lift's shaft when a nearer floor is beside them; a fall that starts just under a floor line begins on it; lifts rest half as long between rides; no fruit bushes in lift shafts.

### 19 November 2026

#### Version 0.14.0

[`2947236d`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/2947236d887b49b057e2a4435f07ab41714ac193)

#### C2 mind: no foraging boat ride back to a shore searched lately

[`b876da2d`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/b876da2d7a1c389e6ebc951d4628d3659e413e5b)

Starving norns rode the east sea's boat back and forth (one five round

search further" never asked whether the far shore had just been searched.
Now a direction is skipped when a learned boat ride that way lands where

OPENC2E_OLD_BOAT_FORAGE restores the old rule.

#### C2 relationships: gifts credited to the giver, sharing and defending

[`c887ea56`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/c887ea569470ccb8dcb781590126354037b4317d)

The owner's relationship ideas (2026-11-11) left to do: share with and
defend friends, and gifts credited to the giver.

Gifts: Agent::drop records who put a thing down and when
(lastDroppedBy, lastDroppedTick). A stimulus felt from a thing a creature
put down within 1200 ticks counts, in Relationships::felt, as from that
creature: eating food a friend carried over warms the eater to it ("brought
me something that eased my hunger"). OPENC2E_NO_GIFT_CREDIT turns it off.

Sharing (HomeostatMind::addShare): a creature not hungry itself (hunger at
most 0.35), with a friend in sight (affinity 0.4 or more) who looks hungry
(0.6 or more; SeenAgent::hunger, the other's Hunger drive), brings it food:
picks some up nearby, carries it over and drops it beside it, the bring
that tending already did (now addBring, shared by both). OPENC2E_NO_SHARE.

Defending (HomeostatMind::addDefend): when a creature is hurt by another,
creatures within 400 px who count it a friend or mate are told who did it
for 200 ticks (oldCreature::defendFrom, MindPerception::defendFrom); unless
they fear that one, are babies, or have more pressing needs, they may go
and hit it. OPENC2E_NO_DEFEND.

Tests: three pairs of 5-minute Home copies (all three on vs off): at hunger
255 127 vs 141, over 200 189 vs 199, meals per thousand 1,569 vs 1,634,
creature hits 1,644 vs 1,503, deaths and faints the same; 3-15 shares and
3-19 defends a copy. A 4-minute copy under AddressSanitizer: clean.
OPENC2E_WATCH_TEND logs share and defend offers.

A fruit bush grew between the cage lift's cables at its middle stop, in

#### C2: a fall starting under a solid floor line begins on that line

[`396d5dfe`](https://github.com/DotairZee/zeetures/commit/396d5dfef631cddf5b415a777b6a140d4c25f9a7)

A walking foot's point lies a few px under its floor line. Creatures
walking east off the cage lift's middle stop (room 21, floor 840, its way
down 120 wide, solid for them) stepped with the front foot onto the tunnel
beside (room 20, its floor about 25 px lower); the step started a fall
with the body's bottom already a few px under 840, so the floor did not
hold it and they fell 320-345 px down the empty shaft, badly hurt (0-6 a
5-minute copy). Now, as snapDownFoot is about to set a creature falling,
if its bottom is up to 12 px under a floor solid at its size under its
middle, it is set back on that floor first, and the fall starts there.

A first version held the body on the middle's floor at every step down
into a new room until the middle passed: six pairs of 5-minute Home
copies cut big falls (142 vs 171) and shaft falls (3 vs 14) but cost 8%
of meals. This one, three pairs: shaft falls 2 vs 8, meals per thousand
558/464/489 vs 498/474/481, falls of 150 px or more 119 vs 124.
OPENC2E_OLD_STEP_HOLD turns it off.
